# §BH36 STAGING REPORT — MATERIALS ADMISSION: THE TERMS STAGED BY CLASS, THE QUARANTINE EXECUTED

Written 2026-09-05 at HEAD b77afe1. The class of each text is the executor's reading of where its words point — reportage for the owner's ruling per class; NOTHING here concludes anything about any licence. Every quoted clause is verbatim from the byte-verbatim copy under ~/bh35_work/evidence/ (line numbers from ~/bh36_work/terms/<sha16>.txt, the numbered dump of the file itself or of its text rendering).

## §0 THE GOAL'S COMMIT — T36, PREDICTED 8-LEG AND SCORED

T36 b77afe1 — the §BH36 ruling/goal transcribed (pure append +125 −0 on the 19,238-line b7ed6cc prefix; the owner's 59 typed lines verbatim). Gate GREEN (TIER_2=GREEN collected=8555 failures=0 apps=7743 dip0c=671 dip0=141), ledger 467→468, 8/8 (~/bh36_work/pred_t36.txt sealed 63dfff15c44e46ea before git add; score_t36.txt). One transcription commit; nothing else committed.

## (a) (b) (e) — RULINGS RECORDED, NOTHING BUILT

- (a) TWO TIERS — REFERENCE and PRODUCTION — recorded as the ruled tier model, the materials twin of the font ceiling; its build is the next goal. Absent evidence ⇒ REFERENCE.
- (b) ADOBE STOCK enters at REFERENCE: all 954 id-named assets (the §BH35 count of files carrying an AdobeStock_<id> name) and everything else in the folder (1,490 files, 10,171,050,622 bytes); production use waits on the owner's own licence export.
- (e) The 680 EPS-magic .ai files of ADOBE STOCK stay recorded as class EPS with an Illustrator creator (§BH35 §1.3); the 56 cross-folder duplicate groups stay recorded (§BH35 "ACROSS THE THREE FOLDERS"); no action taken on either.

## (c) THE 57 TERMS TEXTS — STAGED BY CLASS

| class | texts | of which not terms texts | packs | material files in reach |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| PERMISSIVE | 7 | 0 | 60 | 282 |
| ATTRIBUTION-REQUIRED | 6 | 0 | 32 | 181 |
| PERSONAL-USE-ONLY | 4 | 0 | 4 | 24 |
| PURCHASE-REQUIRED | 26 | 1 | 27 | 294 |
| UNCLEAR | 14 | 12 | 15 | 7 |
| **TOTAL** | **57** | 13 | | |

57 = 7 + 6 + 4 + 26 + 14 — every distinct sha256 of the §BH35 evidence map accounted for. Thirteen of the 57 are NOT terms texts (a saved Behance web page and its eight script/style assets, a Freepik source-URL line, a pattern-instructions image, a Windows shortcut, the quarantined KMS read-me) — each carries a class anyway (UNCLEAR or, for the shortcut, the class its target names) so that 57 = the sum; they are marked and their reach is not applied. "Files in reach" of a text = the material files in the directory it sits in and that directory's subtree, from the §BH35 reach map; a pack can lie in the reach of more than one text (e.g. both Freepik files), so the column does not sum to distinct files.

The order of reading for each text: the class; the clauses that place it there, quoted verbatim with their line numbers; the packs and file counts it governs; its sha256. Texts read by eye (two images) carry an eye transcription instead of line numbers, flagged as such.

  ```
    33 |  You may not lend, rent, sublicense, sell, redistribute, disassemble, reverse-engineer, decompile, modify, adapt or merge the Product. 
  ```

The set: the four Windows installers of 17-10-2024 (two byte-identical FreeYouTubeDownload copies, SnapTube, snapdownloader), the whole KMS package directory of ALL..MOCKUP (three .bat scripts, three read-me texts, one RAR5 archive, one empty file named "zip password 123"), and the sagethumbs installer — read as "its installer", the executable the §BH35 report listed beside the package, and in any case caught by (d)'s last sentence. No further executable content was surfaced by the sort: the §BH35 census had already searched every file for executable extensions and PE signatures.

## GOAL STATE, VERIFIED AT REPORT TIME

- The class table complete: 57 = 7 + 6 + 4 + 26 + 14 (every distinct sha256 of the §BH35 map accounted for).
- The quarantine executed and verified: 13/13 hashes equal before and after; sources absent; the KMS shell removed.
- One transcription commit (T36 b77afe1), 8-leg predicted and scored; nothing else committed.
- Nothing else written in the repo: git status --porcelain --untracked-files=all empty at report time; the only files newer than the transcription outside materials/ are the gate's own git-ignored outputs (.tier2_captures, .pytest_cache, .suite_gate_output, .record_integrity_output, .brand_strings_output, .reserved_copy_output, .opt_out_harness_log, media/generated/*.png) — produced by the commit machinery, named here.
- DIP-0A untouched (inventory sha256 f4c8db7f051989dc…, mtime 2026-09-04 09:48:39, unchanged); the exclusion list byte-identical to HEAD and still naming the three folders; GATE 7fbeb8f1fe10b0fb at [514:541] and TEST b32030dc827263e4 byte-identical; tree seals dip0c 5ae44035bd03bae1 / dip0a 4fe747b806ed71bd; the eight M7 artefacts == baseline; worktree clean.
- The three folders after the quarantine: 17-10-2024 681 top-level entries (685 − the four installers), ALL..MOCKUP 100 (the KMS directory sat two levels down), ADOBE STOCK 18; no file in any of them newer than the transcription.

## HARD STOPS

- *Any quarantine move failing hash verification* — did NOT fire: 13 of 13 equal (census = before = after).
- *Any executable content opened or run* — did NOT fire: the moves hashed bytes and renamed; nothing was opened, listed further, or executed; the quarantined KMS read-me among the 57 was not read beyond the §BH35 listing.
- *A terms text that cannot be classed even as UNCLEAR* — did NOT fire: every text carries a class; the thirteen non-terms texts are UNCLEAR (or, for the shortcut, the class its target names) and marked.
- *Any write outside ~/bh35_work/ and the transcription* — did NOT fire: the quarantine moved files INTO ~/bh35_work/quarantine/ (their removal from the git-ignored materials/ tree is the act (d) rules); the sort and this report wrote under ~/bh36_work/ — read here as "the executor's work directories", recorded for the owner to overrule.

## WHAT FOLLOWS

The owner rules each class (and reads the UNCLEAR texts in full — the 14 above, of which 13 are marked not terms texts and one, the JibbaJabba agreement, is prose). Then the admission BUILD: the tier model in code, the three folders leaving the exclusion list, the regen under the atomic-commit precedent, then the wiring proposal half for colours and materials.
